- **scan-secrets-entropy**: Friction, not a security boundary: asks a person before a write adds secrets scan-secrets misses -- high-entropy values (16-150 chars) by secret-like keys, GitHub/npm tokens with valid checksums, Stripe test keys, Slack/AWS key-id shapes, Luhn-valid cards. Fondé sur l’absurde procédural de Kafka (Le Procès, 1925) : la machine judiciaire ne distingue pas entre un homicide et un TypeError. Le même protocole criminalistique s’applique à tout. Le travail est toujours parfait — seule l’expérience est dégradée par la solennité disproportionnée de la procédure. - **scene-view-planner**: 单张场景/空间图片的机位视角推演：用户只发一张图且信息不足时，先反推这是什么场所的哪个功能分区、其余方向应是什么区并请用户确认；确认后把场景图分析并转成标准黑白俯视 CAD 工程平面图，再由用户在平面图上用箭头指定方向（或说反打/左侧/右侧/某角度），几何推演新机位并生成同一空间对应视角的静态场景图，全部视角出齐后拼 2×2 四宫格总览做位置关系与风格一致性自检。适用于：用户发场景图想要俯视图/平面图/户型机位分析、只给一张图想知道这是什么场景及其他视角长什么样、想要某场景的反打镜头、左侧视角、右侧视角、背面/对面视角、换个方向或角度看同一个场景、在平面图上画箭头指定想看的方向、分镜机位补全、AI 漫剧/短剧/影视化场景的多视角一致性生成等任务。 ([EdwinZDZ/scene-view-planner](https://github.com/EdwinZDZ/scene-view-planner/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **scene-wire**: Drives Unity MCP (`manage_gameobject`, `manage_ui`, `manage_components`) from a natural-language description of a scene/prefab wiring change — creating GameObjects, parenting them, setting RectTransform anchors, adding components, assigning serialized fields, and setting up event wiring. Reads the current scene state first (`find_gameobjects`), plans the smallest set of MCP calls, applies them, and reports what changed. Refuses to hand-edit `.unity` / `.prefab` YAML. - **scene-writer**: Writes a screenplay scene in industry-standard format — slugline, action lines, and dialogue — from a scene brief and character notes, ready to drop into a draft. - **scene-writer**: A skill that writes one dramatic scene slowly. Aware of the design of empty space (what not to say), show-don't-tell, and the seams of the scene, it finishes one scene on the desire → conflict → change beat. To complete one episode as a self-contained short story, it also applies the four-act structure (ki–shō–ten–ketsu), the fleshing-out principle, and flash/short/long scaling. Where fast-draft writes all at once, use this when you want to write one scene deeply. ([GL-Kageyama/soul-voice-teller](https://github.com/GL-Kageyama/soul-voice-teller/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-writer) · ★1)
- **scene-writer**: Draft or rewrite a SAGA scene, chapter, or episode with the reader-onboarding harness and Korean-primary sequence. Use when writing new prose or rewriting a scene.
